import { BlockInfoCard } from "@/components/ui/block-info-card"

<BlockInfoCard type="rootly" color="#6C72C8" />

使用方法 [#使用方法]

Integrate Rootly incident management into workflows. Create and manage incidents, alerts, services, severities, and retrospectives.

ツール [#ツール]

rootly_create_incident [#rootly_create_incident]

Create a new incident in Rootly with optional severity, services, and teams.

入力 [#入力]

| パラメータ              | 型       | 必須  | 説明                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------ | ------- | --- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`           | string  | はい  | Rootly API key                                                                                                                            |
| `title`            | string  | いいえ | The title of the incident (auto-generated if not provided)                                                                                |
| `summary`          | string  | いいえ | A summary of the incident                                                                                                                 |
| `severityId`       | string  | いいえ | Severity ID to attach to the incident                                                                                                     |
| `status`           | string  | いいえ | Incident status (in\_triage, started, detected, acknowledged, mitigated, resolved, closed, cancelled, scheduled, in\_progress, completed) |
| `kind`             | string  | いいえ | Incident kind (normal, normal\_sub, test, test\_sub, example, example\_sub, backfilled, scheduled, scheduled\_sub)                        |
| `serviceIds`       | string  | いいえ | Comma-separated service IDs to attach                                                                                                     |
| `environmentIds`   | string  | いいえ | Comma-separated environment IDs to attach                                                                                                 |
| `groupIds`         | string  | いいえ | Comma-separated team/group IDs to attach                                                                                                  |
| `incidentTypeIds`  | string  | いいえ | Comma-separated incident type IDs to attach                                                                                               |
| `functionalityIds` | string  | いいえ | Comma-separated functionality IDs to attach                                                                                               |
| `labels`           | string  | いいえ | Labels as JSON object, e.g. \{"platform":"osx","version":"1.29"}                                                                          |
| `private`          | boolean | いいえ | Create as a private incident (cannot be undone)                                                                                           |

出力 [#出力]

| パラメータ            | 型       | 説明                              |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------- |
| `incident`       | object  | The created incident            |
| ↳ `id`           | string  | Unique incident ID              |
| ↳ `sequentialId` | number  | Sequential incident number      |
| ↳ `title`        | string  | Incident title                  |
| ↳ `slug`         | string  | Incident slug                   |
| ↳ `kind`         | string  | Incident kind                   |
| ↳ `summary`      | string  | Incident summary                |
| ↳ `status`       | string  | Incident status                 |
| ↳ `private`      | boolean | Whether the incident is private |
| ↳ `url`          | string  | URL to the incident             |

rootly_update_incident [#rootly_update_incident]

Update an existing incident in Rootly (status, severity, summary, etc.).

入力 [#入力-2]

| パラメータ                 | 型       | 必須  | 説明                                                                                                                                       |
| --------------------- | ------- | --- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| `incidentId`          | string  | はい  | The ID of the incident to update                                                                                                         |
| `title`               | string  | いいえ | Updated incident title                                                                                                                   |

| `severityId`          | string  | いいえ | Updated severity ID                                                                                                                      |
| `status`              | string  | いいえ | Updated status (in\_triage, started, detected, acknowledged, mitigated, resolved, closed, cancelled, scheduled, in\_progress, completed) |
| `kind`                | string  | いいえ | Incident kind (normal, normal\_sub, test, test\_sub, example, example\_sub, backfilled, scheduled, scheduled\_sub)                       |
| `private`             | boolean | いいえ | Set incident as private (cannot be undone)                                                                                               |
| `serviceIds`          | string  | いいえ | Comma-separated service IDs                                                                                                              |
| `environmentIds`      | string  | いいえ | Comma-separated environment IDs                                                                                                          |

| `functionalityIds`    | string  | いいえ | Comma-separated functionality IDs to attach                                                                                              |

| `mitigationMessage`   | string  | いいえ | How was the incident mitigated?                                                                                                          |
| `resolutionMessage`   | string  | いいえ | How was the incident resolved?                                                                                                           |
| `cancellationMessage` | string  | いいえ | Why was the incident cancelled?                                                                                                          |

| ↳ `kind`         | string  | Incident kind                      |
| ↳ `summary`      | string  | Incident summary                   |
| ↳ `status`       | string  | Incident status                    |
| ↳ `private`      | boolean | Whether the incident is private    |
| ↳ `url`          | string  | URL to the incident                |
| ↳ `shortUrl`     | string  | Short URL to the incident          |
| ↳ `severityName` | string  | Severity name                      |

| ↳ `resolvedAt`   | string  | Resolution date                    |

| パラメータ              | 型      | 必須  | 説明                                                       |
| ------------------ | ------ | --- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`           | string | はい  | Rootly API key                                           |
| `actionItemId`     | string | はい  | The ID of the action item to update                      |
| `summary`          | string | いいえ | Updated action item title                                |
| `description`      | string | いいえ | Updated description                                      |
| `kind`             | string | いいえ | The kind of action item (task, follow\_up)               |
| `priority`         | string | いいえ | Priority level (high, medium, low)                       |
| `status`           | string | いいえ | Action item status (open, in\_progress, cancelled, done) |
| `assignedToUserId` | string | いいえ | The user ID to assign the action item to                 |
| `dueDate`          | string | いいえ | Due date for the action item                             |

出力 [#出力-34]

| パラメータ           | 型      | 説明                                  |
| --------------- | ------ | ----------------------------------- |
| `actionItem`    | object | The updated action item             |
| ↳ `id`          | string | Unique action item ID               |
| ↳ `summary`     | string | Action item title                   |
| ↳ `description` | string | Action item description             |
| ↳ `kind`        | string | Action item kind (task, follow\_up) |
| ↳ `priority`    | string | Priority level                      |
| ↳ `status`      | string | Action item status                  |
| ↳ `dueDate`     | string | Due date                            |
| ↳ `createdAt`   | string | Creation date                       |
| ↳ `updatedAt`   | string | Last update date                    |

rootly_run_workflow [#rootly_run_workflow]

Trigger a Rootly automation workflow, optionally scoped to an incident or alert.

入力 [#入力-39]

| パラメータ             | 型       | 必須  | 説明                                                                             |
| ----------------- | ------- | --- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `apiKey`          | string  | はい  | Rootly API key                                                                 |
| `workflowId`      | string  | はい  | The ID of the workflow to run                                                  |
| `incidentId`      | string  | いいえ | Incident ID to run the workflow against                                        |
| `alertId`         | string  | いいえ | Alert ID to run the workflow against                                           |
| `immediate`       | boolean | いいえ | Run immediately (true) or respect the workflow wait time (false). Default true |
| `checkConditions` | boolean | いいえ | Whether to evaluate the workflow conditions before running. Default false      |

出力 [#出力-39]

| パラメータ             | 型      | 説明                                                                                 |
| ----------------- | ------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `workflowRun`     | object | The triggered workflow run                                                         |
| ↳ `id`            | string | Unique workflow run ID                                                             |
| ↳ `workflowId`    | string | ID of the workflow that ran                                                        |
| ↳ `status`        | string | Run status (queued, started, completed, completed\_with\_errors, failed, canceled) |
| ↳ `statusMessage` | string | Status detail message                                                              |
| ↳ `triggeredBy`   | string | What triggered the run (system, user, workflow)                                    |
| ↳ `incidentId`    | string | Associated incident ID                                                             |
| ↳ `alertId`       | string | Associated alert ID                                                                |
| ↳ `startedAt`     | string | When the run started                                                               |
| ↳ `completedAt`   | string | When the run completed                                                             |
| ↳ `failedAt`      | string | When the run failed                                                                |
| ↳ `canceledAt`    | string | When the run was canceled                                                          |
